# Golden Canopy - Kazu Guitar Canon Journey

An original hour-long, light-classical instrumental suite inspired by the descending-sequence harmony and interlocking counterpoint of Pachelbel's public-domain Canon in D. New melodies and arrangement.
Kazu Guitar is the lead instrument. Lower-guitar echo enters four bars after the leading theme, with another guitar playing flowing arpeggios; strings, bass, brushed percussion and distant bells fade in and out.
**Duration:** 60:00 exactly in score at 96 BPM / 4/4. **Key:** D major. **Bars:** 1440.
**Musical loop:** The closing passage thins out, settles on dominant A7, and resolves to D major on restart. No written pause or fade-out after the final bar.
**Audio note:** Use a WAV export without the renderer's default +0.5 s tail for precise 60-minute looping.

## Evolving movements
- 00:00 - 01 First Light Through the Window
- 03:20 - 02 The Garden Wakes
- 06:40 - 03 Quiet Footsteps
- 10:00 - 04 Soft Rain and Sunlight
- 13:20 - 05 A Letter in the Breeze
- 16:40 - 06 Lanterns in the Orchard
- 20:00 - 07 Above the Golden Hills
- 23:20 - 08 Blue Sky Interval
- 26:40 - 09 By the Slow River
- 30:00 - 10 Leaves in Motion
- 33:20 - 11 Silver Shoreline
- 36:40 - 12 Summer Clouds
- 40:00 - 13 Wind Over the Meadow
- 43:20 - 14 Courtyard at Dusk
- 46:40 - 15 Paper Constellations
- 50:00 - 16 First Stars
- 53:20 - 17 Homeward Paths
- 56:40 - 18 Return to the Beginning

Five dynamic passages per movement: sparse / gentle / flowing / quick sixteenth-note runs / release.
